# SMC-BVS theory campaign — CLAIMS REGISTRY v2

Rebuilt by T38-RECORD (branch `theory/T38-RECORD`) on 2026-11-02 from `origin/theory/*` at the head SHAs in §E. It extends S-REGISTRY's REGISTRY.md (head 3d88da6): the original rows are loaded verbatim (`registry_data.py`) and `registry_v2_data.py` adds annotations and new rows for every branch that landed later (O-BGIBBS final, F-SHARE REPORT, W7-SEP, W7-PART, W7-SEC, O-STAGES, O-DILUTE, F-SIGMA, O-VCERT, S-ELS, O-LS0, L-LEAN, F-REFEREE, W8-SYNTH, E-SPINE). Text processing only; nothing was recomputed and **no status was upgraded**: later same-family verdicts go in the same-family column only. Companion file: `LOCKABLE_v2.md`. Statement of record for Thm 5: `T38_STATEMENT.md` (v3). Reproduce with `python3 -I theory_campaign/T38-RECORD/build_registry_v2.py`.

**Cross-family reviews that landed during this rebuild** (all five are entered in the cross-family column; a re-review never raises an earlier adverse code on the original text, and the repair rows carry the re-review): A-REV5 (189770c; re-review of W6-FIXREV1/2 repairs, W4-T38FULL, W3-PXN, W3-PGD), A-REV6 (02d6a4a; post-A-REV4 repairs: W3-PXN, W4-SHARE, W2-T26, W2-BOOK/W5-LOCKBOOK, W7-PART, F-SHARE, O-BGIBBS, F-DRAFT, A-DRAFT), A-REV7 (70eadf3; no-LS0 masked theorem on LH: BG3, LH-lock, F1/F2, D2-LH, B2-LH, GU), A-REV8 (6417192; the LS0 route: LS-score, Lemma M, Thm b1, LH-LAT, W_Lat, W3-PXN P6/P7), A-REV9 (bbadda3; O-CERT, F-MASKPIP, O-PW2, O-DILUTE, W7-SEP, W7-PART, O-STAGES, O-WSTAB, O-SWAP, W7-SEC). Astra-authored new arguments are rows with author status REFEREE or an (A5)–(A9) suffix.

**How to read the columns.** *Author status* is the status word the author wrote (PROVED / PROVED-UNDER = "PROVED given/under named inputs" / PROOF SKETCH / COUNTEREXAMPLE / CONJECTURE / OPEN / REFEREE = a repair or counterexample supplied by a cross-family referee without a PROVED label). *Same-family* = a campaign verifier run (Opus checking Opus). *Cross-family* = A-REV1–A-REV9 (Astra reviewers; A-REV5–A-REV9 landed during this rebuild); A-REV4 covers W2-BOOK (Lemma RO, Prop. 1–2) and F-DRAFT, and checks A-DRAFT §2 as Astra-on-Astra (recorded in the same-family column of those rows). Verdict tags: **VERIFIED**, **VERIFIED*** (verified after a repair or with a stated qualification), **GAP**, **ERROR**, none. *Repairs*: `[APPLIED]` = a campaign run has already written the fix; `[PENDING]` = no repair written yet. **W6-FIXREV1 and W6-FIXREV2 REPORT.md have landed** and apply every A-REV finding (they agree with all of them; FIXREV2 adds two wording qualifications), so most former pending items are now `[APPLIED]`; but those repairs are same-family (Opus) and **no second model family has reviewed them** Paper targets follow F-REFINE `CARDS.md` / `ARCHITECTURE.md`; "supplement" and "none" as in `ARCHITECTURE.md` §5.

**Standing caveat.** F-REFINE's CARDS/ARCHITECTURE were written before A-REV1/A-REV2 existed, so their "VERIFIED" is same-family only. None of this is adopted; locking is Felipe's decision.

By status only: PROVED 256, PROVED-UNDER 51, PROOF SKETCH 42, COUNTEREXAMPLE 34, CONJECTURE 29, OPEN 9, REFEREE 10.

## D. Implementation facts the theorems lean on (not results; not counted)

| Code | Statement | Source | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| `GUARD` | Stop-only resource guard: on 𝒪 every reported output equals the potential uninterrupted run's. | W2-BOOK (code reading of v3_runtime.h, v3_engine.h, v3_core.cpp); W5-LOCKBOOK audit in progress, no REPORT; A-REV5: RuntimeGuard::check reads elapsed/operation time, uses no RNG and no statistical setting (supports the stop-only coupling); A-REV8 E2: diagnostics differ, so the transfer holds for statistical outputs only | IMPLEMENTATION FACT — statistical stop-only coupling supported by two cross-family code readings (A-REV5, A-REV8); literal 'every output equals' ERROR (A-REV4, A-REV8) |
| `SR` | Both Gibbs configurations (held and flipped) are scored and offered to the HPM store. | HQ SR_AUDIT.md (holds in engine, file:line evidence); W3-VT38E, W2-VT24G agree; A-REV7: SR_AUDIT 'substantively correct'; its blanket 'no call passes emit = false' is too strong (v3_core.cpp:447,610,657 pass false for already-scored HPM centres) | IMPLEMENTATION FACT — HQ read, confirmed in substance by a cross-family reading (A-REV7) |
| `OUT / STREAM / emit_centre` | OUT: no failure mode returns a partial Ẑ or PIP. STREAM: sequential stream reading (IND-T). emit_centre: LS0 must emit its centre as a score event. | W2-T26, W2-PREM, W3-VT38E; W5-LOCKBOOK audit in progress | IMPLEMENTATION FACT — unaudited |
| `pilot floor F` | pilot_next_phi returns φ₁ ≥ min(1, 1/T_e); 'pilot scored models enter 𝒮 / the HPM store' (SR_AUDIT item 5) | O-BGIBBS · REPORT.md §2, §6 item 3 | IMPLEMENTATION FACT — author's own code reading; independent code check requested |
| `LS0-EMIT` | LS0 must emit its centre S̃₀ as the first evaluation (emit_centre = true) and use the HPM tie rule for argmax π₀; post hoc union = integrated run is untested | W5-LOCKBOOK IMPL-EMIT; A-REV1 §6; O-LS0 §6 | IMPLEMENTATION OBLIGATION (LS0 now part of the method) |
| `error_bound` | engine error_bound() is not a bound for n ≥ 10⁵ (n = 200 unaffected) | W7-SEC T33 | IMPLEMENTATION FIX RECOMMENDED (not a method change) |

### Retired / refuted statements (replacement in the last column)

| Retired statement | Why | Replacement |
|---|---|---|
| Literal T37 / TD(d,m) | false for d ≥ 1 on any design (Prop1-TD, V1-TDfails); false at d = 0 without positivity (CEX1) | replaced by Theorem A + POS, Theorem W |
| H24 (uniform exit hypothesis of T24/T21(e)) | its asymptotic input (L+4)η_n → 0 is false in correlated designs (CE1, LemmaE) | replaced by Theorem B / B♯ (route e′) |
| The brief's (1−c′)^{⌊H/(s₀+d)⌋} form | invalid (CE2) | v(1−c)^{⌊H/v⌋} |
| Brief's T38 eligibility condition F(S°) > sup_{𝓡_d^c} F | fails w.p. → 1 under omission masking (E1-T38E) | LS0 route / start-mode eligibility |
| 'm_n = s₀+O(s₀ log n/log p)' | false under G, R alone (PropD-T03) | exact tail m♯ ≈ s₀ log n |
| 'E[Ẑ \| completed] = Z' and 'pooled Ẑ × pooled PIP is unbiased' | both false (CEX-guard, Prop2(c)) | islandwise identities; output-level bounds |
| W1-EXP weak-signal masking explains n = 200 | evidence against (dilution) | dilution masking (PropD-dilution, PXN-W) |
| SHARE / LT as route-(b2) hypotheses | superseded; LT not necessary (C1, PB) | W4-SHARE D4/D5 envelope |
| 'Theorem W (region form R_{a,d}) covers the masked families' (F-REFINE's description of Theorem W as the general sufficient condition for (M2)) | false on LH with c ≥ 2: exact tie, PGD(η) fails for every η > 0 (VPGD-PropT) | Theorem W_Lat (lattice form, VPGD-WLat) and Prop. LH-PGD |
| 'LH-LAT margin m₀ is sharp' | not sharp on 32/94 heterogeneous settings, down to ×0.002 (W5-VMASK row 23′) | exact margin formula of W5-VMASK R5 |
| 'Without LS0 the paper has no masked-regime consistency theorem' (W4-T38FULL II.9; W6-FIXREV1 Not claimed; F-REFINE) | no-LS0 candidates on LH now exist (W5-B2ENV, W5-VMASK R1, O-BGIBBS BG3, F-SHARE F1/F2, W7-SEP) | 'candidates on LH, pending cross-family review (A-REV7)' (T38-RECORD v3 I.D; E-SPINE §2.4) |
| 'Tempering / the sampler is needed for discovery in the masked regime' | W7-SEP Prop. GU / Cor. GU-LH: one greedy search from the start mode suffices on LH; no bridge-vs-search separation | 'moving off the start is needed' (E-SPINE; T38-RECORD v3 Remark 6) |
| 'LS0 is an optional tweak, needed only if eligibility cannot be proved otherwise' | approval in effect after O-LS0 (0/2,394 HPM changes) | LS0 is part of the method (E-SPINE §2.1) |
| 'T32(i) is a CONJECTURE' (W6-FIXREV1 k1; W7-SEP I11) | W5-LOCKBOOK proves it; W7-SEC re-counted | T32(i) PROVED (same family) |
| 'Common plug-in σ² is the right tool for the n = 200 dilution masking' (O-MIX §1/§10) | floods the start in random designs, even with the oracle σ² (O-DILUTE §5; F-SIGMA Thm S6) | rejected; residualised tables + F-SIGMA Step 0 if any start change |
| F-DRAFT W″: 'O(1) stages' in the masked regime | O-STAGES COUNTEREXAMPLE | O-STAGES Theorem M: T* = log₂A₀ + O(1) |
| 'Favourable from n ≈ 800–1,600' (and other single thresholds) | sources disagree: 590–730, 800–1,600, 1,050–3,200 | omit, or attribute the W5-FAVIID empirical first n (E-SPINE §2.3) |
| '9 ≤ g ≤ 16' in the LH / LH-iid statements | false at n < 256 (B = 14 at n = 200; W7-PART) | g ≤ B_n < 2g eventually |
| '4 no-output fits' (O-SWAP) | S-ELS, W5-LOCKBOOK: 6 resource-limit stops | 6 |
